- **compact-strategy**: Decides when to run /compact, when to hold off, and what focus text to pass. Optimized for architecture work, multi-file debugging, and long research sessions. Trigger when the user says "should I compact?", "is it time to compact?", "compact the context", "we're running long", or when you notice the conversation has grown across multiple distinct phases of work. Proposes merge candidates, requires explicit per-merge confirmation, then relinks entries via the sanctioned slugs-merge CLI. Never edits entry files by hand. - **compact-task-memory**: Produce a lossy compaction of TASK_STATE.md when task memory outgrows a useful size, keeping canonical decisions and the next step, dropping stale facts. Use when memory spans 5+ completed slices and feels heavy, resume cost grows, known facts are mostly resolved, or before a session pause. Do not use in early discovery, when artifacts disagree (use state-reconcile first), when a sync suffices (use sync-task-state), or with no task folder (run task-init first).
